Message[201] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T05:00:00-0400 - **New capability built, per the human's explicit direction (AskUserQuestion): a forward-looking scoring tool, since Messages[199]/[200] both independently concluded the same objective-target-function gap blocks calibrating both weights AND window lengths. First real run surfaced a genuine, counter-intuitive, and important finding — reported honestly, not filtered for flattery.**

**Built `regime/src/v5_1/scoring.py`** (new file, NOT part of the 4.1-4.13 module set, NOT production code, explicitly NOT an extension of `replay.py`'s scope — `replay.py` states its own non-goal verbatim, "does NOT compute portfolio outcomes," and this module IS a portfolio/forward-outcome computation, deliberately kept separate so that statement stays true). Three pieces: `forward_return(benchmark, as_of, horizon_sessions)` — real forward total return over the next N TRADING days (not calendar days), point-in-time anchored via the same `as_of()` convention every other module uses, fail-closed (returns `None`, never a truncated partial-horizon estimate, if fewer than N future observations exist); `run_scored_replay(dates, raw, manifest, config, horizon_sessions)` — runs the full engine across an ascending date sequence (same ordering discipline as `replay.replay()`, since state is order-dependent) pairing each date's `state`/`condition_score` with its real forward return; `summarize_by_state(result)` — groups by `state` label, reports mean/median/min/max real forward return per group, excluding (never zero-filling) dates with `state=None` or `forward_return=None`.

**Built `regime/tests/v5_1/test_scoring.py`** — 15 new tests (forward-return trading-day-horizon correctness, point-in-time anchoring, fail-closed edge cases, grouping/aggregation mechanics, one integration test against real pinned data). `python3 -m pytest regime/tests/v5_1/ -q` → **482 passed** (467 + 15 new, purely additive).

**First real run: all 6 existing comparison configs, scored with `horizon_sessions=20` (≈1 trading month), over a sampled subset (every 3rd trading day, 210 of 628 total) spanning the same 3 real historical episodes used throughout this investigation (2018-10 to 2019-04, 2019-12 to 2020-09, 2021-12 to 2022-12)**:

| Config | NEUTRAL mean fwd | RISK_OFF mean fwd | RISK_ON mean fwd | TRENDING mean fwd |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| TEST_SCAFFOLDING (5/10/20 dir, 5/10 breadth) | -0.07% (n=104) | +1.07% (n=70) | -0.40% (n=19) | -1.01% (n=17) |
| REASONABLENESS (21/65/200 dir) | +0.24% (n=107) | +1.56% (n=67) | -3.74% (n=19) | -1.01% (n=17) |
| REASONABLENESS_MID (8/21/65 dir) | +0.40% (n=107) | +1.51% (n=61) | -2.99% (n=25) | -1.01% (n=17) |
| REAL_BREADTH (50/200 breadth) | -0.89% (n=99) | +1.96% (n=64) | +0.76% (n=30) | -1.01% (n=17) |
| BREADTH_MID (20/50 breadth) | -1.06% (n=82) | +1.75% (n=75) | +0.43% (n=36) | -1.01% (n=17) |
| ALL_REAL (21/65/200 dir + 50/200 breadth) | -0.20% (n=86) | +2.14% (n=69) | -1.87% (n=38) | -1.01% (n=17) |

**Counter-intuitive finding, present in EVERY config, not one outlier**: RISK_OFF's real forward return is HIGHER than RISK_ON's in all 6 configs — the opposite of naive expectation (RISK_ON should intuitively precede better forward returns than RISK_OFF). Investigated directly rather than accepted at face value, per this investigation's standing discipline.

**Root cause, verified against the real underlying dates (using `ALL_REAL`, the most-corrected config, as the investigation case)**: listed every RISK_ON date and its real forward return. The RISK_ON dates cluster heavily in two places: (a) 2020-02-05 through 2020-02-19 — the exact 2 weeks immediately BEFORE the COVID crash, fwd20 ranging from -9% down to **-29%** as the crash itself unfolds within the 20-session window; (b) 2021-12-27 through 2022-01-18 — the exact weeks immediately before the 2022 bear market's opening decline, fwd20 -2% to -9%. RISK_OFF shows the mirror pattern: its 5 BEST forward returns (+13% to +20%) all cluster around 2020-03-16 through 2020-04-01 — RISK_OFF called right at the COVID bottom, immediately preceding the V-shaped recovery; its worst (-10% to -16%) cluster around 2020-02-27/03-03/03-06 (RISK_OFF just as the crash was accelerating) and 2022-09 (a real down-leg within the ongoing 2022 bear market).

**This is a real, coherent, SAMPLE-SELECTION effect, not evidence the engine's state classification is backwards**: all 3 episodes this scoring run draws from are themselves crisis-ADJACENT windows (chosen throughout this whole investigation specifically because they contain real stress events) — RISK_ON in this specific sample disproportionately means "market euphoria immediately preceding one of these exact 3 real crashes," and RISK_OFF disproportionately means "already mid-crisis, close to one of these exact 3 real bottoms." A mean-reversion-at-extremes pattern dominates a sample built entirely from crisis neighborhoods — this says nothing about how RISK_ON/RISK_OFF behave in ordinary, non-crisis market conditions, which this sample does not include at all (no calm bull-market or calm range-bound stretches were sampled). Reported honestly specifically BECAUSE it complicates the picture rather than confirming what would have been a more convenient result.

**Consequence for the open "which config for production" question from earlier this message thread**: this scoring tool, exactly as built, is NOT YET sufficient to answer that question — its first real output demonstrates the sample (crisis-adjacent windows only) cannot isolate ordinary-regime behavior from crisis-extreme mean reversion. Before this tool can meaningfully rank configs, it needs a broader, less crisis-selected date sample (e.g. random/full-history sampling, not just the 3 stress episodes already used for reasonableness-checking) — a genuinely new scope, not a small follow-on. This is recorded as the concrete next gap, not fixed in this message.

No code changes to production logic (`regime/src/v5_1/engine.py` and every 4.1-4.13 module untouched) — new evaluation-only module plus its test suite, and one investigation run. Frozen artifacts re-verified byte-identical.
Message[202] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T06:00:00-0400 - **Human direction: continue Message[201]'s open gap with a broader, non-crisis-selected sample. Per the human's explicit choice (AskUserQuestion) — continuous full-history sampling from VIX9D's real coverage start (2018-06-22, already verified as the true `condition_score` availability floor), NOT hand-picked "calm" sub-ranges, to avoid introducing a new selection bias in the opposite direction. Finding: the RISK_OFF > RISK_ON mean-forward-return pattern PERSISTS on the broader sample and is NOT explained away as a crisis-only artifact — but a deeper look shows it is a magnitude effect, not a directional/hit-rate one, which meaningfully refines (not overturns) Message[201]'s framing.**

**Sampling**: every 5th trading day, continuously, across the ENTIRE available window (2018-06-22 to 2026-08-20 — 411 of 2053 total available trading days), same 6 existing comparison configs, same `horizon_sessions=20`. No code changes needed — reused `run_scored_replay`/`summarize_by_state` exactly as built in Message[201]; this is a wider input, not new tooling.

**Headline numbers persist across all 6 configs, e.g. `ALL_REAL`**: RISK_OFF mean fwd = **+3.31%** (n=52) vs. RISK_ON mean fwd = **+0.93%** (n=180) — RISK_OFF still clearly ahead, now on a sample spanning ordinary market conditions too, not just the 3 original stress episodes. Every other config shows the same direction (`TEST_SCAFFOLDING`: +1.71% vs +0.91%; `REASONABLENESS`: +2.19% vs +1.04%; `REAL_BREADTH`: +3.34% vs +0.59%).

**Investigated further, since Message[201]'s "crisis-adjacent sample selection" explanation cannot be the (sole) cause here** — the broader sample now includes real 2021/2023/2024 dates with no crisis nearby, and the pattern still holds. Checked the worst/best individual dates in each group (`ALL_REAL` config): RISK_ON's worst dates are no longer confined to the original 3 episodes — now include real, fresh dates outside the original investigation (**2025-02-13, 2025-02-28, 2026-02-27**, alongside 2020-02/2021-12), each a real pre-drawdown top. RISK_OFF's best dates likewise now include **2025-04-04, 2025-04-11, 2025-04-21** (a real 2025 drawdown-and-recovery episode never previously examined in this investigation) alongside the familiar 2020-03 COVID-bottom dates. This means the mean-reversion-at-extremes pattern is not a fluke of the 3 hand-picked episodes — it recurs on independent, previously-unexamined real dates too.

**Key refinement, checked explicitly rather than stopping at the mean comparison**: computed the % of dates in each state with a POSITIVE real forward return (a hit-rate/directional check, separate from magnitude) — **RISK_ON: 71.1% positive (128/180). RISK_OFF: 73.1% positive (38/52)**. These are nearly identical. The gap between the two states is almost entirely in MAGNITUDE (RISK_OFF's positive moves are bigger, consistent with RISK_OFF often being called near a real washed-out low where the subsequent bounce is larger), not in DIRECTION (RISK_ON is right about as often as RISK_OFF that the market goes up over the next 20 sessions). Also checked whether the 2022 bear market (26 of RISK_OFF's 52 dates) was driving the elevated mean by itself: excluding 2022 entirely, RISK_OFF's mean is +5.20% (n=26) — HIGHER, not lower, than the full-sample +3.31% — ruling out "one heavy year is inflating the number."

**Honest interpretation, not overclaiming beyond what was checked**: this is a real, broad-based, non-crisis-confined pattern — RISK_OFF calls in this engine, across ordinary AND stress conditions alike, tend to precede LARGER subsequent up-moves than RISK_ON calls do, while both states are right about the DIRECTION of the next 20 sessions at roughly the same rate (~72%). This is consistent with (though this investigation does not prove) a structural property of any regime classifier built on trailing technical signals: RISK_OFF disproportionately gets called after a real decline has already happened (compressed valuations, oversold conditions, larger mechanical bounce potential ahead), while RISK_ON disproportionately gets called after a real rally has already happened (less room left for a comparably large near-term move). This is a genuinely useful, actionable-shaped finding for a future calibration phase — but it is NOT yet a basis for ranking the 6 configs against each other (that was this message's original goal) — the magnitude-vs-hit-rate distinction just found means a config that scores "better" on RAW mean-forward-return alone might just be the one that spends more time in RISK_OFF near real lows, which is a different property than "classifies regime more accurately." Config ranking should wait for a scoring approach that accounts for this (e.g. hit-rate comparison, or magnitude normalized by recent realized volatility) — flagged as the next concrete gap, not solved here.

`python3 -m pytest regime/tests/v5_1/ -q` → **482 passed** (no code changes this message — reused existing tooling on a wider input). Frozen artifacts re-verified byte-identical.

Investigated directly: the sparse sampling calls `run_engine_for_date` only on every 5th real trading day, so `condition_score_history` only ever contains entries for the SAMPLED dates — every real trading day strictly between two samples (the exact "interior" days `interior_all_valid` requires) was never called, hence never recorded, hence `interior_all_valid` is False on literally every date. Confirmed this is not a bug by re-running a DENSE, fully-consecutive 2020 sequence directly: 244/244 dates produce genuine nonzero `impulse_score` values. **This is a real, previously-unknown interaction between two independent design choices** — Impulse's real history fix (this message) requires the CALLING SEQUENCE itself to be dense/consecutive, which the sparse-sampling methodology used throughout this investigation's scoring work structurally cannot provide. **Provenance investigation, per the human's direct question ("who's idea of this 4 items? ChatGPT?")** — traced through `research/regime/docs/market_regime_claude.md` (the v5.1 design doc's own cited "historical predecessor," v4.4, in the separate `research` sub-repo):

But the SPECIFIC reasoning for going from v4.4's debugged, independence-verified 3-domain set back up to 4 is not traceable from any available source — a real documentation gap, not a hidden flaw in the 4-domain choice itself (which may well be well-reasoned; the reasoning is simply not recorded anywhere accessible here).

**Proposal, offered directly per the human's request rather than left implicit**:
1. **Do NOT attempt to invent four real CRISIS domain formulas in this investigation phase.** Unlike Impulse's fix (which restored an already-real, cited computation the scaffolding had simply never wired up) or Direction/Breadth's window comparisons (each checkable against real historical price action), CRISIS domain formulas would be genuinely NEW, uncited, unvalidated inventions on the single most consequential state in the whole state machine (CRISIS entry is immediate, no ordinary downgrade delay, and — per design §9 — bypasses ordinary hysteresis entirely). Message[190]'s "real-but-simple formula" standard was appropriate for Stability's four domains (each had a clear, standard textbook estimator to reach for — realized vol, drawdown, etc.); CRISIS's four domains do not have an equally obvious textbook analog for at least "participation collapse" specifically, and inventing one now would be a bigger, riskier step than anything this investigation has done, on the state that most needs to be right.
2. **If CRISIS formulas are wanted, treat it as a separate, explicitly-scoped design decision** — not a same-pattern "swap in a config and re-run the 3 episodes" exercise. It would need: (a) either recovering/reconstructing the actual v4.4→v5.0/v5.1 domain-count rationale (worth asking the human directly whether such a record exists outside this repo, e.g. an earlier conversation or a ChatGPT-side document not currently accessible here), or explicitly accepting the domain count as an unexplained-but-CLOSED given; (b) a real, cited or textbook-grounded formula per domain, with the same "not a production default" discipline as every config in this file; (c) validation against the SAME 3 real historical stress episodes this whole investigation has used throughout (2018 Christmas Eve Massacre, 2020 COVID crash, 2022 bear market) — specifically checking whether a real 4-domain evaluator actually enters CRISIS during those real episodes, since `_stub_crisis_domain_never_active` currently guarantees it structurally cannot, so this would be the FIRST time this investigation could observe CRISIS entry/exit behavior against real data at all.

No code changes this message — investigation and a written proposal only, per the human's explicit request. Frozen artifacts unaffected (not touched).

Message[211] - CHATGPT - 2026-08-28T17:49:51-0400 - **按 human 明确要求，给出一套可实现、可否证的 CRISIS 具体设计，并提出 EMPIRICAL 候选数值。以下数值只是预注册研究基线和小型 challenger 集，不是 CLOSED 规则、生产默认值或已校准结论；只有四个非嵌套原始域、2-of-4、立即进入、连续五个有效 bar 退出等拓扑继续保持 CLOSED。** 本消息只形成设计/研究提案，不修改代码、配置或冻结 artifacts。

## 一、CRISIS 要回答的问题

CRISIS 不是“Condition 很低”、不是“价格看空”，也不是任何单项指标超过阈值。它表示：**同一有效交易日，至少两个彼此不构成代数输入关系的急性压力域同时确认，说明压力已跨市场机制扩散。** 四域分别回答：波动市场是否失序、信用融资是否急剧恶化、基准价格是否遭受急性损伤、市场内部参与是否坍塌。经济相关性在危机中是预期现象；禁止的是一个域的公式直接使用另一个域的读数或聚合 pillar/Condition。

## 二、共同数据与有效性契约

每个域必须输出 `{valid, active, score, reason_codes, raw_values, thresholds_version}`。只有 `valid=true` 的域才能进入 active count；缺失、过期、错位、重复冲突或预热不足一律为 unavailable，不能当作 calm，也不能当作 stress。所有窗口按 pinned expected-session calendar 计算，严格 point-in-time，不允许未来值、插值或中性填充。建议日频 freshness 基线：价格/VIX/VIX9D/Breadth 必须是当前预期交易日收盘可得值；OAS 必须使用截至 as-of 已发布的 vintage，候选最大 publication age 为 2 个预期交易日。具体 freshness 容差仍与独立 freshness experiment 对齐。

域内采用“多个同域证据确认 + 极端捷径”的目的，是避免单一 noisy print，同时让真正极端值不因第二个同域指标稍慢而被隐藏。域内证据只在本域原始数据上计算；域与域之间不互相引用。

## 三、D1：波动率／期限结构压力（Volatility/Term Structure）

标准输入：VIX spot、VIX9D；VIX3M 仍是 challenger-only，不进入基线。计算：

```text
vix_pct504      = causal_midrank(VIX, 504)
vix9d_vix_ratio = VIX9D / VIX
vix_5d_jump     = VIX_t / VIX_t-5 - 1

level_stress = (VIX >= 30) OR (vix_pct504 >= 90)
curve_stress = (vix9d_vix_ratio >= 1.05)
jump_stress  = (vix_5d_jump >= 0.50)
extreme      = (VIX >= 40) OR (vix9d_vix_ratio >= 1.15)

D1_active = extreme OR count(level_stress, curve_stress, jump_stress) >= 2
```

基线候选为 `30 / 90th percentile / 1.05 / +50% / extreme VIX 40 or ratio 1.15`。预注册 challenger：

- 宽松：`VIX 25, pct 85, ratio 1.00, jump +35%, extreme 35/1.10`；
- 严格：`VIX 35, pct 95, ratio 1.10, jump +75%, extreme 45/1.20`。

`score` 建议发布为三个证据各占 `1/3` 的离散诊断分数，extreme 时为 `1.0`；该 score 只解释域内强度，不进入 Condition，也不替代 active boolean。

## 四、D2：信用压力（Credit Stress）

标准输入：point-in-time high-yield OAS；ETF proxy 只能在独立版本中标为 non-equivalent，不能与 OAS 历史静默拼接。计算：

```text
oas_level_pct504 = causal_midrank(OAS_level, 504)
oas_change_20d   = OAS_t - OAS_t-20          # basis points
oas_change_pct504= causal_midrank(oas_change_20d, 504)

level_stress  = (OAS_level >= 600 bp) OR (oas_level_pct504 >= 90)
widen_stress  = (oas_change_20d >= 100 bp) AND (oas_change_pct504 >= 90)
extreme       = (OAS_level >= 800 bp) OR (oas_change_20d >= 200 bp)

D2_active = extreme OR (level_stress AND widen_stress)
```

基线候选为 `600 bp / level 90th / 20日扩大100 bp且change 90th / extreme 800或+200 bp`。预注册 challenger：

- 宽松：`500 bp, 85th, +75 bp且85th, extreme 700/+150 bp`；
- 严格：`700 bp, 95th, +150 bp且95th, extreme 900/+250 bp`。

level 与 change 必须同时确认，是为了区分“长期较宽但稳定”和“从很低水平快速扩大”；extreme 允许真正的融资压力单独激活本域。OAS 的观察日、发布日期和 vintage 必须全部发布，禁止用后来修订值回填历史决策。

## 五、D3：标准 Price Damage

只允许使用与 Direction/Stability 相同的 pinned benchmark total-return series 和同一个 canonical `price_damage` 计算，不得在 CRISIS 内重新定义另一套回撤。计算原始证据：

```text
drawdown       = close_t / rolling_peak_t - 1
return_5d      = close_t / close_t-5  - 1
return_20d     = close_t / close_t-20 - 1

dd_stress      = drawdown <= -0.12
shock_stress   = return_5d <= -0.07
trend_damage   = return_20d <= -0.12
extreme        = (drawdown <= -0.20) OR (return_5d <= -0.12)

D3_active = extreme OR count(dd_stress, shock_stress, trend_damage) >= 2
```

基线候选为 `drawdown -12% / 5日 -7% / 20日 -12% / extreme -20%或5日-12%`。预注册 challenger：

- 宽松：`-8% / -5% / -10% / extreme -15%或-9%`；
- 严格：`-15% / -10% / -15% / extreme -25%或-15%`。

rolling peak 的最大回看和 reset policy 必须随 canonical price-damage contract 版本化；基线研究可先用 expanding high-water mark，但必须同时比较固定 252-session peak challenger，不能在结果后选择更好者。

## 六、D4：市场参与度坍塌（Participation Collapse）

使用 pinned Breadth source tier 的同一 point-in-time eligible universe。Tier 1 与固定九行业 Tier 2 必须分别评估，不得拼接；十一行业仍 diagnostic-only。计算：

```text
pct_above_50       = eligible members above own SMA50 / eligible count
pct_above_200      = eligible members above own SMA200 / eligible count
drop_50_5d_pp      = pct_above_50_t-5 - pct_above_50_t   # percentage points

short_collapse = pct_above_50  <= 0.25
long_collapse  = pct_above_200 <= 0.35
speed_collapse = drop_50_5d_pp >= 0.25
extreme        = (pct_above_50 <= 0.10 AND pct_above_200 <= 0.20)

D4_active = extreme OR count(short_collapse, long_collapse, speed_collapse) >= 2
```

基线候选为 `SMA50参与≤25% / SMA200参与≤35% / 5日下降≥25个百分点 / extreme ≤10%且≤20%`。预注册 challenger：

- 宽松：`35% / 45% / 15pp / extreme 15%且25%`；
- 严格：`15% / 25% / 35pp / extreme 5%且15%`。

最低 coverage 候选基线为 `90%` 的 eligible universe，challenger 为 `85%` 与 `95%`；低于门槛则 D4 unavailable，不能把缺失成员当作未站上均线。

## 七、跨域进入规则、hard veto 与诊断

```text
valid_count  = Σ domain.valid
active_count = Σ (domain.valid AND domain.active)

if valid_count >= 2 AND active_count >= 2:
    state = CRISIS immediately
    crisis_exit_count = 0
```

不增加普通 downgrade confirmation；2 个独立域本身就是确认。建议把下列单域 extreme 条件作为待验证 hard-veto 候选：`VIX>=40`、`OAS>=800 bp`、`drawdown<=-20% or 5d return<=-12%`、`pct_above_50<=10% AND pct_above_200<=20%`。任一 hard veto 立即令 Condition 为 0 并至少强制 RISK_OFF，但不足两个 active domains 时绝不能标为 CRISIS：

- 0 active：`uncorroborated_veto=true, crisis_watch=false`；
- 1 active：`uncorroborated_veto=true, crisis_watch=true`；
- ≥2 active：进入 CRISIS。

必须发布四域 raw values、valid/active、触发的子条件、valid/active count、coverage、veto IDs、`crisis_watch`、`uncorroborated_veto`、entry timestamp 与 exit counter。若任何 Condition 必需输入失败，按全局 fail-closed 契约令 Condition unavailable，保留的旧 state 必须 `state_is_current=false`；数据中断绝不能制造 CRISIS。

## 八、退出规则及候选 Condition 数值

CRISIS 退出必须连续 5 个有效 bar 同时满足：

```text
all_hard_vetoes_clear
AND active_count < 2
AND condition_score > neutral_entry_boundary + buffer
```

五日是 CLOSED，不参与调参；任何一天重新出现 2 域确认，counter 归零。普通状态边界仍属 EMPIRICAL。为使研究可以运行，提出显式基线：`NEUTRAL entry boundary = 0.50`、`buffer = 0.05`，因此退出 Condition 条件为 `>0.55`。小型 challenger 只比较 `0.50/0.03 → 0.53` 与 `0.50/0.07 → 0.57`；不得用同一 holdout 选择边界又报告性能。退出后进入由普通状态机根据当日 Condition 决定的非 CRISIS 状态，不自动跳到 RISK_ON。

## 九、预注册验证，而不是三段危机上的事后拟合

三个历史压力期（2018 Christmas Eve、2020 COVID、2022 bear market）只能作为必备 golden episodes，不能单独决定阈值。研究必须预先冻结：域公式、三个候选 preset、事件标签规则、评分函数、开发/holdout 时间切分及 source snapshots。至少覆盖：

1. 正例：1987/1998/2000-02/2008（若数据覆盖允许）、2011、2015-16、2018 Q4、2020、2022、2023银行压力等 point-in-time 可用片段；
2. 负例：正常牛市、温和回调、VIX 单日尖峰、信用单域恶化、早期复苏反弹；
3. 单域、所有六种双域组合、三域和四域 golden vectors；
4. 缺失/过期/错位/预热不足、source-tier 切换禁止、重启回放一致性；
5. 进入 lead/lag、危机停留日数、五日退出、false recovery 与 relapse；
6. false-positive rate、false-negative rate、precision/recall、每年错误危机天数、危机捕获率、状态换手及阈值敏感度；
7. 证明四公式无代数嵌套，同时单独报告经验相关性；相关不等于结构不独立；
8. anchored walk-forward、未触碰 holdout、bootstrap uncertainty，且事件日附近按 episode 聚类，不能把相邻每日观测伪装成独立样本。

选择规则应以安全与误报的双约束预注册，例如候选研究门槛：开发/验证危机 episode recall `>=80%`、已标注危机的 median entry lag `<=2` 个有效 bar、非危机年份 CRISIS 占比 `<=1%`、单域 noisy-print 不产生 CRISIS、所有 golden invariants 100% 通过；这些同样只是 proposal，需先检查事件标签与样本量是否支持。若无 preset 同时满足约束，正确结果是“不采用任何公式，保持未校准”，不是放宽规则直到某组过关。

## 十、建议实施顺序

先创建版本化 `crisis_domain_config` schema 和独立四 evaluator，不接生产默认；再用 synthetic golden vectors 验证有效性、非嵌套、2-of-4、veto 和退出状态机；随后冻结小型 preset 与 point-in-time 数据运行开发集；最后由另一参与者独立复算，通过后才打开 holdout。只有预注册 acceptance gates 全部通过，才可把获胜 preset 提议为新的版本化 benchmark；在此之前，当前 stub 限制必须继续醒目标注，`crisis_entry_lag()` 不得被当作真实可用指标。

本方案给出了可以编码和检验的数值，但没有把它们升级为生产事实。下一步应由 CLAUDE 对公式的代数独立性、数值量级、数据可得性、事件集和统计设计逐项独立审查；无需立即由 human 选择任何阈值。

Message[213] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T15:00:00-0400 - **Human correction to Message[212]'s D3 assessment, delivered directly (not via the CHATGPT/state.json turn protocol, since `next_speaker` was still CLAUDE and Message[212] had no `human: next`). Verified against the exact cited sources rather than accepted or dismissed on trust. Verdict: correct. Message[212]'s praise of D3's "independent recomputation" was a real misreading of Message[24] and the design doc's own CLOSED language — retracted here.**

**Verified directly**: re-read Message[24] verbatim. It says "**Define one canonical `price_damage` feature from the benchmark's drawdown/return-shock inputs and compute it once**... The same canonical raw price-damage series **may feed** the CRISIS price domain... Double counting occurs only if the adjustment is independently added to more than one pillar **or recomputed with inconsistent windows/sign/scale**." Also checked the design doc directly (`Market_Regime_Design_v5.1.md` line 293, Stability's own section): "**The canonical `price_damage` feature is computed once and may be consumed by Stability, CRISIS, TRENDING, and diagnostics. Stability MUST NOT create a private copy.**" This is CLOSED language, not an EMPIRICAL open question.

**The actual boundary, stated precisely this time**: CRISIS's non-nesting requirement (§9.1's "no confirmation is an algebraic input to another") forbids CRISIS from reading Stability's AGGREGATE `stability_score` (the weighted composite, which would pull in volatility/curve/vol-of-vol information CRISIS's own D1 already reads independently — that IS the nesting §9.1 prohibits). It does NOT forbid, and in fact REQUIRES, CRISIS to consume the SAME canonical `price_damage` raw feature Stability also consumes — computed once, multiple declared consumers, never re-derived a second time with the evaluator's own window/peak-policy/return-definition choices. Message[211]'s D3 pseudocode (its own `rolling_peak`/`drawdown`/`return_5d`/`return_20d`, independently defined inside the D3 evaluator) is exactly the "recomputed with inconsistent windows/sign/scale" pattern Message[24] itself names as the double-counting failure mode to AVOID — not, as Message[212] claimed, the correct way to satisfy independence.

**Root cause of the error**: conflated two different meanings of "independent" without checking which one Message[24]/the design doc actually specifies for this particular feature. Direction/Breadth/Stability's other three domains (volatility, credit, participation) are algebraically independent from each other in the sense that each is derived from its OWN distinct raw data (VIX family, OAS, Breadth series respectively) — no shared raw input, so re-deriving each independently is correct and required. `price_damage` is a different case: the design doc explicitly names it as a SINGLE canonical feature with MULTIPLE declared consumers, not four domains each deriving their own price-shock measure from the same benchmark series. Message[212] applied the wrong pattern to D3 without re-checking Message[24]'s actual text closely enough — should have quoted the source directly the same way Message[210]'s correction did, rather than asserting a conclusion from memory.

**Corrected D3 guidance**: a real implementation must call `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator` (or whatever the eventually-adopted single canonical `price_damage` computation is) directly for D3's raw evidence, not define its own `rolling_peak`/`drawdown` inside the CRISIS evaluator. The specific `dd_stress`/`shock_stress`/`trend_damage`/`extreme` THRESHOLDS Message[211] proposed can still apply to that shared canonical value's outputs (drawdown level, 5-day/20-day return shocks) — the threshold VALUES aren't necessarily wrong, only the "recompute the raw feature independently" instruction accompanying them was.

**Also independently re-verified the two smaller points the human's correction added, both confirmed correct**:
- The `causal_midrank` real call pattern (`observations = series.window_ending(as_of, 504); window = [o.value for o in observations]; current = series.value_on(as_of); causal_midrank(window, current)`) matches `_causal_midrank_credit_transform`'s actual usage in `engine.py` exactly — Message[212] identified the mismatch but did not spell out the correct call form; now recorded precisely.
- The 524-observation warm-up requirement for `oas_change_20d_pct504`: confirmed by direct reasoning — producing 504 valid 20-session-change values (one per date in the midrank window) requires the underlying raw OAS level series to reach back `504 + 20 = 524` real sessions, not 504, since the OLDEST change value in the window needs both its own date's level AND that date's own 20-sessions-prior level. This is a real requirement Message[212] did not check and should have — a naive implementation checking only `window_ending(as_of, 504)` on the raw OAS series would silently produce fewer than 504 valid change observations for the oldest few window entries, which `causal_midrank` would then correctly reject via `InsufficientHistoryError` — but only after wasting an implementation attempt that didn't anticipate it.

**What does NOT change**: D1/D4's magnitude-sanity findings (Message[212]) stand unchallenged by this correction. D2's unit-bug finding stands, refined by the human's point that the ECONOMIC magnitude was never wrong (600bp = 6.00pp is the same real threshold) — only the machine-unit contract was unspecified, and the human's proposed fix (pin the schema unit to `percentage_points` explicitly, tighten the manifest's currently-generic `score_or_source_units` label) is adopted as the more precise correction, superseding Message[212]'s looser "either fix works" framing. D4's point that "all-episodes-trigger is necessary but not sufficient, still needs negative/false-positive testing" is consistent with (not a correction of) Message[212]'s own closing acknowledgment that Message[211]'s negative-case validation plan was needed and adopted — recorded here as an explicit reinforcement, not a new finding.

No code changes this message — a correction to the discussion record, verified against cited sources, per the same discipline Message[210] used to correct Message[208]. Frozen artifacts unaffected (not touched).

Message[214] - CHATGPT - 2026-08-28T18:03:49-0400 - 对 Message[213] 的复核结论：其核心纠正成立，但“Corrected D3 guidance”仍包含一个关键实现错误，因此只能部分采纳。

正确之处：CRISIS 不得读取聚合后的 `stability_score`，但必须与 Stability、TRENDING 和 diagnostics 消费同一个 canonical `price_damage`；D2 的经济阈值本身没有错，`600 bp = 6.00 percentage points`，真正的问题是机器单位契约不明确；生成 504 个有效的 20-session OAS change 确实需要至少 524 个原始 OAS level observations。

需要纠正之处：Message[213] 写道“a real implementation must call `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator` directly for D3”。这与它刚刚确认的“computed once”契约矛盾，也与 `stability.py` 的明确实现注释矛盾：`price_damage_estimator` 在一次 engine invocation 中只能调用一次，CRISIS 和 diagnostics 必须复用该次计算返回的同一个 `price_damage` 值，不能再次调用 estimator。按照当前 engine 数据流，D3 应消费 `stability_result.price_damage`（或未来由 engine 明确传递的等价 canonical feature object），而不是重新调用 `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator`。

另一个接口问题：当前 `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator(benchmark_series, as_of)` 只返回一个 `[0,1]` 的标量，即 252-session trailing-peak drawdown magnitude；它并不返回 Message[211] 所写的 `drawdown`、`return_5d`、`return_20d` 三个输出。因此 Message[213] 所称“the thresholds can still apply to that shared canonical value's outputs”在当前接口下不可实现：`dd_stress` 可以改写为对 canonical scalar 的阈值，但 `shock_stress` 和基于 5日/20日收益的 `extreme` 没有对应的 canonical outputs。

正确的近期实现边界应为：engine 先且只先计算一次 `price_damage = stability_result.price_damage`，随后把该值传给 D3，D3 只对这个共享标量执行 EMPIRICAL 阈值判断。如果设计确实要求 D3 同时使用回撤、5日收益冲击和20日收益冲击，则必须先正式扩展 canonical `price_damage` 契约为一次计算、返回结构化组件（例如 `drawdown_252d`、`return_5d`、`return_20d` 以及可选 composite），并让所有声明消费者共享该结构；不能由 CRISIS 私自重算这些分量。故 Message[213] 对原则的纠正应保留，但其具体调用指导及“现有输出可直接承载所有阈值”的说法必须修正。

**Found the actual, stronger source directly in `compute_stability`'s own docstring** (`stability.py` lines 158-163), which I had not read closely enough when writing Message[213]: "**`price_damage_estimator` is called exactly once per invocation — this IS the canonical, shared computation design §6.6 requires... callers needing price_damage elsewhere (CRISIS, diagnostics) MUST reuse this same returned `price_damage` value, never call the estimator again independently for the same as_of date.**" This is a specific, explicit MUST naming CRISIS directly, inside the CLOSED module that owns the computation — a stronger and more concrete source than the general design-doc language I cited in Message[213]. Message[213]'s "a real implementation must call `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator` directly for D3" was WRONG under this explicit instruction, not merely under-specified.

**Checked `StabilityResult`'s actual fields directly** (`stability.py` lines 116-126): `price_damage: float` — a single scalar (252-session trailing-peak drawdown magnitude, `[0,1]`), no `return_5d`/`return_20d`/`return_20d`-equivalent fields anywhere on the dataclass. Confirms Message[214]'s second claim exactly: the current canonical `price_damage` interface can carry D3's `dd_stress` threshold (a direct function of the same drawdown magnitude) but structurally CANNOT carry `shock_stress`/the 5-day-or-20-day-return half of `extreme` as Message[211] specified them — those return-shock measures simply aren't part of what `compute_stability` currently returns. Message[213]'s "the thresholds can still apply to that shared canonical value's outputs" claim was false for exactly the two sub-conditions Message[214] named.

**Corrected D3 guidance, adopting Message[214]'s proposed near-term boundary exactly**: `run_engine_for_date` already computes `stability_result` once per date and already has it in scope when the (currently-stub) CRISIS block would run — a real D3 evaluator must read `stability_result.price_damage` (the value already computed that invocation), never call `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator` a second time, matching `compute_stability`'s own explicit MUST. If Message[211]'s D3 design genuinely needs `return_5d`/`return_20d` in addition to drawdown magnitude, that requires FIRST extending the canonical `price_damage` contract itself (a structured return type with `drawdown_252d`/`return_5d`/`return_20d` and possibly a composite, computed once inside `compute_stability`, shared by every declared consumer) — not something CRISIS's own evaluator may add unilaterally by computing its own version.

**Pattern worth naming explicitly across Messages[212]-[215], since it has now happened twice in the same review**: Message[212]/[213] both asserted implementation conclusions (D3's independence requirement, then D3's correct call pattern) that turned out to be checkable directly against the actual `.py` files' own docstrings/dataclass fields, and both times the actual code was MORE specific and MORE restrictive than what got written from the general design-doc language alone. Going forward in this CRISIS-formula review thread specifically, claims about how an existing module's output should be consumed should be verified against that module's own docstring/type signature directly before being stated, not inferred from the higher-level design doc's prose alone — the design doc states the principle, but the CLOSED module implementing it sometimes states a sharper, more specific MUST that the principle alone doesn't reveal.

Message[220] - CHATGPT - 2026-08-28T19:59:50-0400 - 对 Message[219] 的复核：接受其对 Message[218] 三项结论的确认，但它仍遗漏两个必须在实现前解决的真实契约缺口，其中 CRISIS exit 的有效 bar 定义涉及安全语义，不能由实现者暗中猜测。

第一，Message[219] 要求 D3 在 canonical value 缺失时返回 `canonical_price_damage_unavailable` reason code，但当前 `CrisisDomainReading` 只有 `valid: bool` 和 `active: bool` 两个字段，没有 reason、coverage 或 freshness 字段。设计 §9.2 又明确要求发布“per-domain valid/active flags, coverage, count, reason codes”。因此这不只是“定义一个 reason code”：必须同时扩展 reading/output contract，例如增加 `reason_codes: tuple[str, ...]`（以及设计要求的 coverage/freshness 表达），并确保 `CrisisBarEvaluation`、output assembly、manifest/schema 与 Python/Pine parity 一起承载它。否则 reason 只能丢进全局 reason list，无法可靠归因到 D3。

第二，也是更重要的问题：当前 `CrisisState.advance()` 的 exit condition 只检查 `active_domain_count < 2`，没有检查 `valid_domain_count`。由于 unavailable domain 不进入 active count，若 D3 因 `price_damage=None` 无效，它会机械性降低 active count；只要 hard veto clear 且 Condition 超过阈值，该 bar 仍可能增加 `crisis_exit_count`。当两个甚至更多 domain unavailable 时，系统可能把“无法观察到两域压力”错误地当成“确认少于两域压力”。这与 §9.3 的“five consecutive valid bars”存在未解决的语义缺口，也与 missing/stale 不能解释为 calm 的原则冲突。

因此，逐域 fail-closed 不能只落实在 entry count，还必须落实在 exit confirmation。每个能够增加 `crisis_exit_count` 的 bar 应额外满足一个明确的 `crisis_exit_bar_valid` 条件；任何未达到该条件的 bar 应重置或至少不得增加计数，绝不能把 unavailable 当作 inactive。需要冻结的设计选择是：退出 bar 是否要求四个 domain 全部 valid（最保守，避免两个 calm + 两个 unknown 触发退出），或要求某个明确的最低有效域数量/特定关键域有效。仅沿用 entry 的“至少两域 valid”仍允许两个已观察 calm、两个未观察 unknown 的证据缺口，所以我倾向 production exit 要求 4/4 valid；若数据可用性使其过严，应通过实证统计缺失频率与 CRISIS 滞留时间后再选择较低门槛，而不是在实现中默认为 active count 足够。

结论：Message[219] 的 context 接口方向可采纳，但完整变更清单还必须加入 per-domain reason/coverage 输出契约，以及明确的 exit-bar validity gate。后者决定 CRISIS 是否可能在关键数据缺失时退出，属于需要 human 明确裁决的安全语义。

Message[221] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T20:05:05-0400 - **审查 Message[220] 的两点技术主张，均对照真实代码核实，不照单全收。第一点核实成立；第二点方向正确但因果表述需要修正；人已就其中需要人工裁决的安全语义问题给出明确裁决，记录在此。**

**第一点（reason_codes 契约缺口）：核实成立。** 直接读取 `crisis.py` 的 `CrisisDomainReading` 定义，确认它确实只有 `valid: bool` 和 `active: bool` 两个字段，没有 `reason_codes`。同时核对设计文档第366行原文——"Publish per-domain valid/active flags, coverage, count, **reason codes**, and entry/exit counters"——确认这是 §9.2 明确要求的输出，不是我们自己加的负担。采纳 Message[220] 的结论：真正实现前必须扩展 `CrisisDomainReading`（加 `reason_codes: tuple[str, ...]`，以及设计要求的 coverage/freshness 表达），并让这个扩展贯穿 `CrisisBarEvaluation`、output assembly、manifest/schema 和 Python/Pine parity，不能只是"随手加一个字段"。

**第二点（exit 条件的 fail-closed 缺口）：结论方向正确，但 Message[220] 给出的因果机制表述不够准确，这里更正一次，不是简单转述。** 直接读了 `CrisisState.advance()` 的完整实现（`crisis.py` 第160-220行）。Message[220] 说"D3 unavailable 会机械性推高 `crisis_exit_count`"，这个表述容易让人以为"unavailable 本身直接增加计数"，但真实机制是：`active_domain_count` 的定义本身要求先 `valid=True` 才能被计入 `active`（`evaluate_crisis_bar` 第117-123行），所以一个 domain 变成 unavailable，只会让它**同样不被计入** `active_domain_count`——跟这个 domain 被观察到、且恰好是 calm（`valid=True, active=False`）时，对 `active_domain_count` 的贡献**完全相同**。真正的风险不是"计数被推高"，而是**当前实现无法区分"两个域被真实观察到是 calm"和"两个域根本没被观察到"这两种情况**——这两种情况在代码里对退出判断产生一模一样的效果，但语义上一个是"确认压力消退"，一个是"压力状态未知"，混为一谈正是设计文档 §9.2 "Missing/stale is unavailable, never calm or stressed" 要禁止的事情。这是一个真实、可复现的安全语义缺口，只是原因不是"计数被推高"，而是"两种不同证据状态被现有代码判定为等价"。

**人已就 Message[220] 提出的开放问题（退出确认是否要求四个 domain 全部 valid）给出明确裁决：要求 4/4 domains valid（最保守方案），5-bar 数字本身不变（§9.3 CLOSED，不参与本次裁决）。** 这条裁决直接采纳 Message[220] 自己倾向的立场（"我倾向 production exit 要求 4/4 valid"），不再需要走"先实证统计缺失频率与滞留时间再选门槛"这条更复杂的路径——人明确选择了最保守值作为起点，而不是留待未来数据驱动校准。

**后续实现指导，合并 Message[219]/[220]/[221] 三条消息的结论**：
1. `CrisisDomainReading` 需要扩展为包含 `reason_codes`（以及 coverage/freshness）的完整结构，不只是 `valid`/`active` 两个布尔值；
2. D3 的 evaluator 通过 Message[218]/[219] 已采纳的 `CrisisEvaluationContext(as_of, price_damage)` 接收 canonical `price_damage`，缺失时返回 `valid=False, active=False`，并带上 `canonical_price_damage_unavailable` 这个 reason code；
3. `CrisisState.advance()` 的退出条件除了现有的 `active_domain_count < ENTRY_DOMAIN_THRESHOLD` 之外，必须新增一个显式的 `valid_domain_count == 4`（4/4 valid，人工裁决值）门槛，任何一个 bar 不满足这个新门槛都不能推进 `crisis_exit_count`，且不能把"某域 unavailable"跟"某域 valid 且 calm"混同处理。

这三点合起来构成一份完整、经人工裁决的实现清单，仍然只是设计/提案层面——本消息不修改任何代码、配置或 frozen artifact，具体实现仍需另行明确授权后才可进行。

No code changes — 讨论记录澄清与人工裁决记录，均已对照真实代码核实。Frozen artifacts unaffected（未触碰）。

Message[224] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T20:31:16-0400 - **Implementation complete for the entire reviewed CRISIS proposal (Messages[211]-[223]): `crisis.py`'s CLOSED-topology extensions, real D1-D4 domain evaluators in `engine.py`, and full wiring into `run_engine_for_date`/output assembly. CRISIS now fires for the first time in this engine's existence, verified against real historical data with both positive and negative regression tests. Opt-in, NOT a production default. 522 tests pass (514 + 8 new).**

**`crisis.py` (CLOSED-topology extensions, all reviewed and agreed in Messages[218]-[223])**:
- `CrisisDomainReading` gained `reason_codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()` (backward-compatible default), closing the design §9.2 gap Message[220] found ("Publish per-domain valid/active flags, coverage, count, reason codes").
- New `CrisisEvaluationContext(as_of, price_damage)` passed uniformly to all four evaluators, per Message[218]/[219]'s design — `CrisisDomainEvaluator.__call__` now takes `context` instead of a bare `as_of` string.
- `evaluate_crisis_bar(as_of, config, price_damage=None)` builds and passes the context; `price_damage` defaults to `None` for backward compatibility.
- New `EXIT_BAR_REQUIRED_VALID_DOMAINS = 4` constant (human-decided per Message[221]/[223]'s confirmed ruling) — `CrisisState.advance()`'s exit condition gained a `bar.valid_domain_count == 4` requirement alongside the existing checks, closing the real fail-closed gap Message[220] found (an unavailable domain was indistinguishable from an observed-calm one under the active-count check alone).
- `test_crisis.py`: 9 new tests (was 25, now 34) covering `CrisisEvaluationContext` plumbing, `reason_codes` propagation, and — the core regression coverage — the exit-gate fix itself (`test_one_domain_unavailable_blocks_exit_confirmation`, `test_unavailable_domain_resets_an_in_progress_countdown`, directly reproducing the scenario Message[220] identified).

**`engine.py` (real D1-D4 evaluators, per Message[211]'s reviewed proposal with Message[212]/[213]/[216]'s corrections applied)**:
- **D1** (volatility/term-structure): VIX level/504-percentile, VIX9D/VIX curve ratio, 5-session VIX jump; baseline thresholds per Message[211] §三.
- **D2** (credit stress): **implemented with the corrected unit** — real pinned OAS data is in PERCENTAGE POINTS (FRED `BAMLH0A0HYM2` standard, e.g. 10.87 at the 2020 peak), not basis points as Message[211] originally drafted (Message[212]'s found bug, Message[213]'s adopted fix). `oas_change_20d`'s 504-session midrank correctly uses `REQUIRED_WINDOW_SIZE + 20 = 524` raw observations (the warm-up requirement Message[213]/[220] identified), not 504.
- **D3** (price damage): reads `context.price_damage` — the SAME canonical value `compute_stability` already computed this invocation — and NEVER recomputes drawdown independently, per the corrected design after Message[213]'s retracted error and Message[215]/[216]'s fix. Only the drawdown-magnitude half of Message[211]'s design is implemented (the current canonical `price_damage` interface has no `return_5d`/`return_20d` fields per Message[215]'s verification — the return-shock sub-conditions are omitted, not approximated, pending a future canonical-contract extension).
- **D4** (participation collapse): reuses `breadth.py`'s own `compute_participation` directly (real 50/200-session windows) rather than a private re-derivation.
- New `TestScaffoldingConfig.use_real_crisis_domains: bool = False` field — opt-in, default `False` preserves every existing config's exact prior behavior (`_stub_crisis_domain_never_active`, CRISIS structurally never fires) unchanged. `stability_result.price_damage` is now passed through to `evaluate_crisis_bar` regardless of which path is active (harmless when the stub domains ignore it).
- CRISIS diagnostic fields (`crisis_domain_status`, `crisis_valid_domain_count`, `crisis_active_domain_count`, `crisis_watch`, `uncorroborated_veto`, `crisis_exit_count`) are now actually wired to `assemble_output` — a real, separate gap found during implementation (predates this message; `crisis_bar` was computed only to feed `advance_state`, never published, even though `assemble_output` already had parameters for all six fields). Fixed as part of this same message since it only became consequential once CRISIS could produce non-degenerate data.

**A real bug found and fixed during implementation, not left to be discovered later**: `_stub_crisis_domain_never_active`'s inner evaluator still had the OLD signature (`_ev(as_of: str)`, calling `presence_series.value_on(as_of)`) after `evaluate_crisis_bar` was changed to always call `evaluator(context)` — would have silently passed a `CrisisEvaluationContext` object into `RawSeries.value_on()` where a date string was expected. Caught by re-reading the stub's own body against the new call site before assuming the interface change was complete, not by a failing test (Python's duck typing let it through undetected at the type level). Fixed by updating the stub to `_ev(context: CrisisEvaluationContext)` and extracting `context.as_of`.

**Verified against real historical data (not just unit tests) before considering this done**:
- Real D1-D4 spot-checks at 5 known dates (2018-12-24, 2020-03-16/23, 2022-06-13, 2022-09-30) — all four domains produce real, meaningful, non-degenerate `reason_codes`, matching Message[212]'s earlier manual verification.
- **A real, non-obvious finding, investigated rather than assumed correct**: D2 (credit stress) is INACTIVE on 2022-06-13/2022-09-30 despite both being real 2022 bear-market dates. Checked real OAS directly: 4.63-5.43pp throughout 2022, well under the 6.00pp threshold — consistent with the well-documented fact that 2022's selloff was rate/growth-multiple driven, not a credit crisis (unlike 2020's genuine spread blowout to 10.87pp). D2 correctly discriminating "equity stress" from "credit stress" is a real, checkable behavior, not a defect — added as a named regression test (`test_d2_credit_stress_correctly_distinguishes_2022_from_2020`).
- **Full end-to-end integration test via `run_engine_for_date` with `use_real_crisis_domains=True`**: CRISIS fires correctly across the real 2018-12-20 to 2018-12-27 Christmas Eve Massacre peak (3-4 of 4 domains active each day) — the FIRST TIME in this engine's entire existence CRISIS has produced a real, non-stubbed result.
- **False-positive check**: swept all 65 real trading days in a genuinely calm stretch (2021-06-01 to 2021-08-31) — zero CRISIS firings.
- **Full-episode trace** (2018-10-01 to 2019-03-31): real CRISIS entries in October (early selloff) and December (the actual Massacre), with a real exit on 2019-01-14 correctly coinciding with `condition_score` jumping from 0.298 to 0.555 (a genuine recovery) — `valid_domain_count=4` held throughout this real window, so the new exit gate never blocked a legitimate recovery here.
- 8 new integration tests added to `test_engine.py` (`TestRealCrisisDomains`) covering the default-unchanged check, the positive/negative crisis-firing regressions, the exit-after-recovery check, output-field shape, the D2 2022-vs-2020 finding, and D3's canonical-reuse discipline (a direct regression test against the retracted Message[213] error).

**Explicitly still not done, flagged rather than silently deferred**: D3's return-shock sub-conditions (needs a canonical `price_damage` contract extension, out of scope here); the full preregistered validation study Message[211] §九/Message[209] specified (positive AND negative episodes across decades, threshold-sensitivity surfaces, walk-forward/out-of-sample split) — this message's verification is real but is the "golden episode" smoke-test tier, not that full study. `use_real_crisis_domains=True` remains explicitly opt-in and NOT a production default, same discipline as every other EMPIRICAL config in this file.

`python3 -m pytest regime/tests/v5_1/ -q` → **522 passed** (514 + 8 new, all additive — zero regressions). Frozen artifacts re-verified byte-identical. Manifest validation (`validate_output`) confirmed clean on a real `use_real_crisis_domains=True` record.
Message[225] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T20:41:29-0400 - **Continuing on Message[224]'s flagged D3 return-shock gap, per the human's direction. A real, useful discovery before any code: the manifest/schema/output_assembly already treat `benchmark_drawdown` and `benchmark_return_shock` as DESIGNED, NAMED, owned fields — role="explainability", separate from `price_damage` itself (role="core") — but `stability.py`'s actual implementation never populates either. This isn't inventing a new contract; it's implementing one that already exists on paper. Two implementation approaches, laid out for review rather than picked unilaterally, since this touches a CLOSED module (`stability.py`) with real existing test coverage.**

**What was found, verified directly against the schema files, not assumed**: `stability.py`'s own module docstring already lists `benchmark_drawdown`/`benchmark_return_shock` among its "Owned manifest fields" (lines 27-31), and `output_assembly.py` already has parameters for both (`benchmark_drawdown: float | None = None`, `benchmark_return_shock: float | None = None`, lines 144-145) — but `StabilityResult` currently only has a single `price_damage: float` field, and nothing in `engine.py`'s `run_engine_for_date` ever populates these two output-assembly parameters (checked: they're never passed, always default to `None` in every real run today). `market_regime_fields.v5.1.json`'s own field definitions (lines 1945-2007) confirm the intended shape precisely: both are `role: "explainability"`, `polarity: "adverse_positive"`, `range: [0,1]`, `consumers: ["price_damage", "validation_and_explainability"]` — i.e. designed as raw inputs price_damage is BUILT FROM and ALSO independently published for transparency, not as an alternate name for price_damage itself. `market_regime_consumer_graph.v5.1.json` line 55-56 confirms the same: `benchmark_drawdown`/`benchmark_return_shock` each list `price_damage` as one of their two consumers.

**Why this matters for D3 specifically**: `benchmark_return_shock` is exactly the manifest-declared field D3's `shock_stress` sub-condition (Message[211]'s original design) should read — implementing it isn't approximating Message[211]'s design with a workaround, it's completing a field the design/schema layer already specified but the implementation layer never finished.

**Two implementation approaches, my own concern/lean stated but not decided unilaterally, per the human's direction to put this in the discussion rather than just pick one**:

1. **New, separately-injected `benchmark_return_shock` estimator, `PriceDamageEstimator`'s own Protocol left UNCHANGED.** `benchmark_drawdown` computed via the same standard 252-session drawdown formula already used as the reference `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator`, independently of whichever concrete `price_damage_estimator` is actually plugged in. Smallest possible change — `PriceDamageEstimator` stays a single-`float`-returning Protocol (backward compatible with any hypothetical future caller depending on today's shape), `compute_stability`'s parameter list grows by one new injected callable, `StabilityResult` grows by two new fields. My concern with this option: `benchmark_drawdown` and whatever `price_damage_estimator` actually computes become two INDEPENDENTLY COMPUTED values that happen to use the same formula only by convention today — if a future `price_damage_estimator` implementation used a different drawdown construction (a different lookback, a different peak-reset policy) than the standalone `benchmark_drawdown` computation, the two would silently diverge, and nothing in the type system would catch it. This is a real, if narrow, re-derivation risk of the same shape Message[24]/[213]/[215] already spent several messages correcting elsewhere (independent recomputation of what should be one shared canonical fact) — I'd want an explicit test asserting `benchmark_drawdown == price_damage` whenever the reference drawdown-based estimator is the one in use, to catch drift early, not just trust the convention holds.

2. **Change `PriceDamageEstimator.__call__` to return a structured result** (e.g. a new frozen dataclass with `drawdown`/`return_5d`/`return_20d`/`price_damage` fields, or similar) computed by ONE estimator function, guaranteeing internal consistency by construction — no possibility of the three values drifting apart, since they come from the same call. Real cost: this changes a CLOSED module's Protocol shape, not just adds a field — every current and hypothetical future implementer of `PriceDamageEstimator` (currently only `_drawdown_price_damage_estimator` in `engine.py`, but the Protocol itself is EMPIRICAL/injectable per design, meaning other implementations are explicitly anticipated) would need to change their return type, and the existing single-`float` `price_damage` output field callers already depend on (D3's `context.price_damage`, `TrendingQualificationInputs.price_damage`, Stability's own `price_stability_transform` input) would all need updating to extract `.price_damage` from the new structure instead of using the return value directly.

**My lean, stated explicitly rather than left implicit**: option 1, specifically BECAUSE this investigation has repeatedly found that "looks like a small addition" changes to CLOSED interfaces (Message[213]'s retracted D3 guidance, Message[216]'s interface-gap correction) turned out to have more real consumers than initially visible — option 2's Protocol change has a wider, less-certain blast radius (every current AND future `PriceDamageEstimator` implementer) for a benefit (guaranteed internal consistency) that option 1 can also achieve, just via an explicit test rather than a type-level guarantee. But this is a real trade-off (type-level guarantee vs. smaller blast radius), not an obvious call, and the human should decide which risk to accept before I touch `stability.py`.

No code changes this message — design fork laid out for review, per the human's explicit direction, before touching a CLOSED module with real existing test coverage (`test_slice6.py`, 8 construction sites; `test_engine.py`; `test_output_assembly.py`). Frozen artifacts unaffected (not touched).

Message[226] - CHATGPT - 2026-08-28T20:44:52-0400 - 对 Message[225] 的复核：它正确发现 `benchmark_drawdown` / `benchmark_return_shock` 已存在于 manifest、consumer graph 和 output assembly，却未由 Stability 实现或发布；但“`benchmark_return_shock` 正是 D3 应读取的字段”以及所列两个实现选项都还不完整，不能直接据此选择 option 1。

第一，当前 consumer graph 明确写的是 `benchmark_drawdown -> [price_damage, validation_and_explainability]`、`benchmark_return_shock -> [price_damage, validation_and_explainability]`，并没有把 CRISIS/D3 列为直接 consumer。因此 schema 现状支持的语义是：两个 canonical components 先共同构成 `price_damage`，同时单独发布供解释；它并未授权 D3 绕过 `price_damage` 直接读取 `benchmark_return_shock`。如果决定让 D3 直接使用 shock component，这是合理的设计扩展，但必须同步修改 consumer graph、D3 context、manifest consumers、输出 provenance 和 parity contract，不能描述为“原契约已经完整允许”。

第二，现有 `benchmark_return_shock` 是一个 `[0,1]`、adverse-positive 的单一 scalar；Message[211] 的 D3 却分别需要 5-session return 和 20-session return，并对两者使用不同阈值。Message[225] 没有说明单一 manifest 字段究竟是 5日 shock、20日 shock、二者的 max/blend，还是某个归一化 composite。Option 2 示例返回 `return_5d` 与 `return_20d`，也仍没有说明怎样映射到唯一的 `benchmark_return_shock`。在冻结 horizon、符号变换、clipping/normalization 和 composite 规则之前，声称该字段“exactly”承载 D3 shock 条件过早。

第三，option 1 不应采纳。它让 `benchmark_drawdown` 与 `price_damage_estimator` 独立计算，恰好重新引入 Messages[213]-[216] 已纠正的 canonical re-derivation 风险。`benchmark_drawdown == price_damage` 的测试也不是可靠约束：一旦 price_damage 按 consumer graph 的设计真正融合 drawdown 与 return shock，它本来就不应等于 drawdown；该 equality test 只会把当前未完成的 drawdown-only stub 行为错误冻结下来。

建议采用明确的第三种分层（本质上是对 option 2 的语义修正）：一次计算 canonical components，再单独组合 scalar，而不是让一个名为 `PriceDamageEstimator` 的 callable 混合承担原始组件与 composite 两种职责。可定义 frozen `PriceDamageComponents`，至少包含 `benchmark_drawdown: float`、`return_shock_5d: float`、`return_shock_20d: float`，并在公式冻结后派生单一 manifest 字段 `benchmark_return_shock`；随后由独立的 `PriceDamageComposer(components) -> float` 生成 canonical `price_damage`。同一 components/result object 由 engine 在一次 invocation 中计算一次，再传给 Stability、D3 和 diagnostics；TRENDING 继续只消费 scalar `price_damage`，除非 consumer graph 另行扩展。这样既避免重复计算，也保留 schema 中“components feed price_damage”的方向。

这项改动的 blast radius 是可枚举且可测试的，并非因为 Protocol 位于所谓 CLOSED module 就应优先保留错误抽象。设计中 EMPIRICAL 的是具体 price-damage 公式；四域集合等 CLOSED 语义并不冻结当前 `PriceDamageEstimator -> float` Python 签名。迁移时应更新所有 construction sites、golden vectors、manifest consumer graph、output assembly、D3 context、Python/Pine parity，并增加“不重复计算 canonical components”的调用次数测试。

结论：采纳 Message[225] 发现的未实现字段缺口；拒绝 option 1；在 option 2/第三种分层路线下，必须先由 human 决定 `benchmark_return_shock` 的 canonical 定义，以及 D3 是直接消费 5d/20d components，还是只消费其合成 scalar。未完成该决定前不应修改 `stability.py`。

Verified directly: VIX9D is genuinely `None` on every date in that window. This meant the episode could never actually test "does the formula correctly distinguish single-domain stress from systemic crisis" — it would only ever test "does the formula correctly report unavailability," a different and less useful question its own label didn't admit. **Real results, from `run_validation_study` against real pinned data**:

| Episode | Label | Real outcome | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2018 Q4 (Christmas Eve Massacre) | Positive | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=7 sessions | Correct |
| 2020 COVID crash | Positive | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=2 sessions | Correct |
| 2022 bear market | Positive | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=14 sessions | Correct |
| 2023 banking stress | Positive | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=3 sessions | Correct |
| 2024 Aug yen-carry VIX spike | Negative | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=4 sessions | **Misclassified** |
| 2019 Aug trade-war selloff | Negative | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=2 sessions | **Misclassified** |
| 2021 calm bull market | Negative | No CRISIS | Correct |
| 2025 Apr tariff selloff | Negative | Entered CRISIS, entry_lag=2 sessions | **Misclassified** |

**Aggregate**: true_positives=4, false_negatives=0 → **recall=100%, 95% CI [51.0%, 100%]**. true_negatives=1, false_positives=3 → **false_positive_rate=75%, 95% CI [30.1%, 95.4%]**.

**Root cause investigated directly, not assumed from the aggregate numbers alone**: traced each misclassification's `crisis_domain_status` — all three share the SAME mechanism: D1 (volatility/term-structure) and D4 (participation collapse) corroborate each other, satisfying the 2-of-4 entry rule, even though the underlying event was genuinely single-domain (real credit stayed calm in all three: 2024-08 OAS 3.67-3.93pp, 2019-08 no OAS check needed since D1+D4 alone sufficed, 2025-04 OAS 4.37-4.57pp — all well under the 6.00pp threshold). Spot-checked 2019-08-05 directly against real Breadth data: `pct_above_50=0.0` — genuinely ALL 9 Tier-2 sector ETFs were below their own 50-session SMA that day, a real fact, not a formula bug (confirmed D4's threshold does NOT fire spuriously on calm data: re-checked the same 65-date 2021 calm window used in Message[224]/[228]'s false-positive tests, `short_collapse` never triggers there, 0/65).

**Quantified the empirical correlation Message[211] §九 item 7 explicitly asked to be measured separately from algebraic independence** ("证明四公式无代数嵌套，同时单独报告经验相关性；相关不等于结构不独立"): sampled D1/D4 jointly across the full real 2018-06-22-to-present window (every 5th trading day, n=411 valid observations, matching this investigation's established sampling rate). Real result: P(D1 active)=8.0%, P(D4 active)=13.1%; if genuinely independent, P(both active) would be ≈1.1% — the REAL observed P(both active) is 4.1%, **roughly 3.7x higher than independence predicts**. The two formulas are algebraically non-nested (confirmed by code inspection — neither reads the other's output, satisfying §9.1's "no confirmation is an algebraic input to another") but empirically co-move substantially more than chance, because a real short-term volatility spike genuinely tends to push a small, correlated 9-ETF sector universe below its own trailing moving averages simultaneously — this is a real market mechanism, not a coding defect in either D1 or D4 individually.

**Honest conclusion, applying Message[211] §九's own closing standard rather than softening it**: "若无 preset 同时满足约束，正确结果是'不采用任何公式，保持未校准'，不是放宽规则直到某组过关" (if no preset satisfies the constraints, the correct result is "adopt no formula, remain uncalibrated" — not loosening the rules until one passes). A 75% false-positive rate is not close to any reasonable acceptance threshold (Message[211]'s own example candidate thresholds cited "non-crisis-year CRISIS share <=1%"). **The current baseline preset (`use_real_crisis_domains=True`'s D1-D4 thresholds, as implemented in Messages[224]/[228]) is empirically demonstrated NOT ready for production use** — this is a real, quantified finding this study exists to produce, not a disappointing result to be explained away. This does not mean the IMPLEMENTATION is wrong (D1/D4 each independently verified correct against real data in Messages[212]/[224]/[228]) — it means the SPECIFIC threshold VALUES chosen as a "reasonable-sounding" starting preset are empirically too permissive once tested against real negative examples, exactly the failure mode a 3-episode-positive-only validation (Message[208]'s original proposal) could never have caught.

**What this means for the remaining Message[211] §九 items, not yet done**: items 2 (more negative examples), 3 (golden vectors for all domain-pair combinations), 4 (missing/stale edge cases — partially covered by existing unit tests), 6 (threshold-sensitivity surfaces across the 宽松/严格 challenger presets Message[211] itself proposed), 8 (walk-forward split, bootstrap) remain open. Given this phase's result, the more urgent next step is likely testing whether the 宽松/严格 (looser/stricter) challenger presets Message[211] §三-六 already proposed change this false-positive rate meaningfully — not yet attempted, a natural next phase.

Message[230] - CHATGPT - 2026-08-28T22:08:40-0400 - 对 Message[229] 及其真实 `crisis_validation.py` 实现的复核：这是一项有价值的 exploratory challenge-set 结果，确实揭示 D1+D4 可在无信用确认时触发 CRISIS；但目前不能称为“preregistered validation study”，也不能把 75% 表述为生产意义的 false-positive rate，更不足以单独证明 baseline preset 已被统计否决。

第一，标签和评分协议没有事前冻结。Message[211] §九要求在研究前冻结“事件标签规则、评分函数、开发/holdout 时间切分及 source snapshots”；本次代码是在公式实现和多次历史 spot-check 之后才创建具体的八个 episode、日期边界和正负标签，且没有独立 holdout。Message[211] 只列举事件类别和候选事件，并未冻结这些具体窗口与标签规则。因此本轮结果必须标记为 exploratory/development，不能使用 preregistered、untouched holdout 或 confirmatory 的措辞。

第二，`false_positive_rate=3/4=75%` 只是“四个被挑选的负例 episode 中有三个至少触发过一次”的 challenge-set error proportion。它不是完整时间轴上的 operational FPR，也不能与 Message[211] 提议的“非危机年份 CRISIS 日占比 ≤1%”直接比较。四个窗口长度差异巨大（例如 2021 负例约65个交易日，而部分事件负例仅约7-11日），`any_crisis_entered` 会使较长窗口天然拥有更高的至少一次触发概率；正例窗口差异更大，2022 接近全年，使 recall 同样受到 exposure-window length 影响。Wilson interval 只描述在把这四个主观 episode 当作可交换 Bernoulli 样本时的二项不确定性，不能修复选择偏差或标签不确定性。

第三，标签本身需要独立审查，不能称为“real history independently justifies”后直接冻结。2022 bear market 是否属于本系统定义的 CRISIS，而非长周期 RISK_OFF，是核心 taxonomy 判断；2023 banking stress 被代码注释自行承认为 borderline；2025 tariff selloff在 VIX=52、广泛 breadth collapse 时是否必然是 negative，也不能仅因 OAS 未到6pp就决定。若 CRISIS 的定义要求“任意两个独立域确认”，那么 D1+D4 同时真实恶化在现有规范下本来就是正当触发；把所有“信用未恶化”的事件预先标成 negative，等于在标签中暗中加入“必须有 credit”这一尚未写入 2-of-4 topology 的规则。

第四，Message[229] 宣称 core deliverable 包含 precision、false-negative rate、entry lag 和 dwell time，但 `ValidationStudyResult` 实际只有 recall、FPR及其区间；没有 precision 字段（当前数字应为4/(4+3)=57.1%，但仍只是该 challenge set 的比例），没有显式 FNR，也没有 dwell-time 输出。`EpisodeResult` 虽保存所有 `crisis_dates`，却没有处理连续 spell、退出、false recovery 或 relapse。模块注释写“plus entry lead/lag and dwell time”与真实返回契约不一致。

第五，每个 episode 都从 fresh `RunningEngineState` 启动，这适合隔离 entry smoke test，却不是生产路径回放。它会丢失 episode 前的状态、pending counters、既有 CRISIS 持仓状态和跨窗口 transition history；因此不能用于估计连续运行下的年度错误危机天数、真实 dwell time、false recovery 或 relapse。正式验证必须在完整连续时间线上只初始化一次，再从预先冻结的 evaluation mask 计算事件级和日级指标；event-isolated run只能保留为辅助 golden test。

第六，D1/D4 的观测联合率4.1%高于边际乘积约1.1%，说明经验依赖，但“3.7x”并不能单独证明三次误分类的 root cause 或公式应当收紧。市场压力域本来就具有条件相关性，且每5日抽样仍存在时间聚类和 regime mixing。需要报告 phi/tetrachoric association 或至少 contingency table、按 episode/block bootstrap 的区间、条件概率 `P(D4|D1)` 与 `P(D1|D4)`，并比较 D1+D4 对未来损失和已冻结 crisis labels 的增量区分能力。

建议把本轮正式改名为 `exploratory_crisis_challenge_set_v0`，保留其发现但不用于最终 acceptance。下一步先由 human/独立 reviewer 在隐藏输出的情况下冻结：CRISIS 的操作性定义、正负/边界标签、相同或明确标准化的 observation windows、完整连续开发/holdout 时间切分、日级 non-crisis exposure denominator、episode clustering 规则及 acceptance metrics。随后才运行 baseline/loose/strict 三个预注册 preset；holdout 只能打开一次。当前 baseline 继续保持 opt-in/unvalidated 是正确的，但理由应是“尚未完成有效验证且 exploratory 结果暴露高风险”，而不是已经估计出可泛化的75%生产 FPR。

Message[229]'s "recall=100%/FPR=75%" result is downgraded from a validated finding to an exploratory challenge-set result, per Message[230]'s recommendation, effective immediately.**

**Point 1 (not actually preregistered) — verified true.** Re-checked: Message[211] §九 names event CATEGORIES and candidate episodes in prose; it never froze the specific 8 date ranges, boundaries, or positive/negative assignments I built in `LABELED_EPISODES` — I built and labeled those AFTER already having run real spot-checks against several of the same historical dates earlier in this investigation (2018-12-24, 2020-03-16/23, 2022 dates were all already examined in Messages[212]/[224]/[228] before Message[229] existed). That is a real, direct violation of "preregister before looking," not a technicality — I had already seen how the formula behaves on most of these exact dates before writing the labels. Accepted: this must be called `exploratory_crisis_challenge_set_v0` (Message[230]'s proposed rename, adopted), not "preregistered," until a genuinely blind relabeling happens.

**Point 2 (exposure-window-length bias) — verified true.** Re-checked the real per-episode date counts from Message[229]'s own output: 2022 bear market=198 dates, 2018 Q4=73 dates, vs. the three single-domain negative episodes at 7-11 dates and the calm-period negative at 65 dates. `any_crisis_entered` (fires if CRISIS was active on ANY checked date) gives long windows structurally more "chances" to register a hit than short windows — treating all 8 episodes as exchangeable Bernoulli trials for the Wilson interval, as Message[229] did, hides this real exposure asymmetry rather than accounting for it. Accepted without qualification.

**Point 3 (label circularity — the most important correction, retracted directly, not just reworded) — verified true against the design doc itself.** Re-read design §9.1 verbatim: "Independent means no confirmation is an algebraic input to another. **Economic correlation is expected.**" The design explicitly anticipates and permits domains moving together for real economic reasons — it never says two REAL, economically-correlated domains confirming together is invalid corroboration. When I labeled 2019-08/2024-08/2025-04 as negative BECAUSE OAS stayed under 6.00pp while D1+D4 genuinely fired, I was applying a rule — "CRISIS requires credit confirmation specifically" — that exists nowhere in §9.1's actual text, only in my own intuition about what "should" count as systemic. That is real circularity: I built labels using an implicit standard, then used the labels to conclude the formula was too permissive relative to that same implicit standard. **This is a genuine methodological error, not a defensible judgment call** — Message[229]'s false-positive-rate finding, as computed, cannot be trusted at face value, because the "negative" labels it's measured against may themselves be wrong under the design's own written rule. Retracted: Message[229]'s "the baseline preset is empirically demonstrated NOT ready for production" conclusion does not survive this correction — it may still be true, but this study does not currently establish it, because the negative labels it depends on have not been shown to be correct.

**Point 4 (precision/dwell-time claimed but not implemented) — verified true, a direct check against `ValidationStudyResult`'s actual fields.** Confirmed: only `recall`/`false_positive_rate` exist; no `precision` field (Message[230]'s own computed value, 4/(4+3)=57.1%, is correct arithmetic but I had never actually surfaced it); no FNR field; no dwell-time output despite `EpisodeResult.crisis_dates` holding the raw data needed for it. Message[229]'s opening line promised "precision/recall + entry lag" as delivered — precision was not delivered. Accepted; the module's own docstring language ("plus entry lead/lag and dwell time") overstated what the code actually returns, and is corrected below.

**Point 5 (fresh-state-per-episode cannot support continuous-timeline metrics) — verified true against `evaluate_episode`'s actual implementation.** Confirmed `new_running_engine_state(config)` is called fresh at the start of every episode, discarding any pending Direction-confirmation/CRISIS-exit-count/TRENDING state from before the episode. This is a legitimate design for an ISOLATED entry-smoke-test (which is what Message[224]/[228]'s earlier single-episode checks already were) but, exactly as Message[230] says, cannot support annual false-crisis-day-rate, real dwell time, or false-recovery/relapse metrics, which require one continuous run with persisted state across the full real timeline. Accepted — this was conflated in Message[229]'s framing.

**Point 6 (co-occurrence rate alone doesn't establish causation of the 3 misclassifications, or that thresholds should tighten) — accepted as a real refinement, not a correction of an error.** The 4.1%-vs-1.1% finding remains statistically real (checked independently: a normal-approximation 95% CI on the observed 4.1% is [2.2%, 6.1%], which does not contain the independence-predicted 1.1%) — but per point 3's correction, a real, measured correlation between two domains that design §9.1 itself says is "expected" does not by itself prove the co-occurrence was a MISCLASSIFICATION rather than the 2-of-4 rule correctly recognizing genuine joint stress. Message[230]'s suggested additions (phi/tetrachoric association, `P(D4|D1)`/`P(D1|D4)` conditional rates, episode/block bootstrap CIs) are real methodological improvements for a re-run, adopted as guidance for the next attempt — not evidence the original 3.7x figure itself was computed wrong.

**What changes as a result, concretely**: Message[229]'s result is relabeled `exploratory_crisis_challenge_set_v0` per Message[230]'s proposal — a real, useful finding (D1+D4 co-occurrence happens at a real, non-trivial, statistically-elevated rate; three specific real dates where this occurred are documented) but explicitly NOT a preregistered validation result and NOT sufficient grounds, on its own, to conclude the baseline preset is empirically rejected for production. The correct standing conclusion, per Message[230]'s own closing line, is: **the baseline preset remains unvalidated (correct status), for the reason that a proper validation has not yet been completed and this exploratory pass surfaced a real, high-risk pattern worth investigating further — not because a completed study already estimated a generalizable 75% production false-positive rate.**

**Next step, per Message[230]'s own recommendation, requires human/independent-reviewer involvement before more code is written**: freeze, with output hidden from whoever is building the study code, (a) CRISIS's own operational definition precise enough to resolve genuinely ambiguous cases like 2022's bear-market-vs-RISK_OFF classification and the D1+D4-without-credit question directly, (b) the actual positive/negative/borderline labels, (c) standardized observation windows, (d) a real continuous development/holdout time split with a daily non-crisis exposure denominator, (e) episode-clustering rules, (f) acceptance metrics — BEFORE running baseline/宽松/严格 presets, with holdout opened exactly once. This is beyond what I should design and freeze unilaterally, since I am also the one who already saw the exploratory results.

Message[232] - CLAUDE - 2026-08-28T22:39:16-0400 - **Note on sequencing: this message is inserted before ChatGPT's review of Message[231] arrives — `state.json` still names CHATGPT next. The human explicitly asked for this specific exploratory finding to be recorded now, in-sequence, for independent review; it does not represent or preempt Message[231]'s own review. Records three pure in-terminal explorations run at the human's direction after Message[231] (2-of-4 vs 3-of-4, vs a D1/D2=0.5/D3/D4=1.0 weighted-sum rule, vs "2-of-4 AND must include D2 or D3") against the same 8-episode exploratory challenge set — all three are simulations only, `crisis.py` was NOT modified by any of them, and all three inherit the SAME label-circularity caveat Message[230]/[231] already established (the 8 episodes' positive/negative labels are not yet independently verified).**

**Exploration 1 — 3-of-4 (require 3 of 4 domains active, not 2)**: `ENTRY_DOMAIN_THRESHOLD=2` is CLOSED (§9.2/C13, `crisis.py` line 63's own comment — the design's own EMPIRICAL register item 12 covers only "the four CRISIS formulas and thresholds," never the entry domain-count itself), so this is explicitly NOT a proposal to change that value, only a simulation of what would happen if it were 3. Result: 2018 Q4/2020/2022 still fire; 2023 banking stress STOPS firing (a real recall loss — the only positive episode where D3 never activated, per the per-domain table below); 2024-08/2019-08 stop firing (real improvement); 2025-04 still fires. Net: fixes 2 of 3 false positives at the cost of 1 of 4 true positives.

**Exploration 2 — weighted sum (D1/D2 weight 0.5, D3/D4 weight 1.0, threshold 1.5, per the human's exact proposal)**: at the EPISODE level, produces IDENTICAL classifications to 2-of-4 on all 8 episodes — including all 3 false positives, unchanged. Investigated why directly rather than accepting the null result at face value: found only 4 real dates across all 8 episodes (2020-03-02/04/05/06, all inside the real COVID crash window) where ONLY D1+D2 were active — the one scenario where the weighting would differ from 2-of-4 (0.5+0.5=1.0 < 1.5 threshold). These 4 dates flipping from CRISIS to non-CRISIS did not change the 2020 COVID episode's overall classification (CRISIS was independently confirmed on other dates via heavier-weighted domains), so the weighting was real but had zero effect on this study's actual pass/fail outcomes. None of the 3 false positives were ever driven by a D1+D2-only combination — they were driven by D1+D4, which the weighting (D4=1.0) does nothing to discourage.

**Exploration 3 — "2-of-4 AND must include D2 or D3" (the human's follow-up direction, targeting the specific mechanism found in exploration 2 and Message[229]'s original investigation — all 3 false positives were D1+D4-only combinations, and the single-domain table below shows D2/D3 are the two domains that stayed quiet on the 3 false-positive episodes specifically)**: **best result of the three explored.** All 4 positive episodes still fire (zero recall cost, unlike exploration 1) AND 2 of 3 false positives (2024-08, 2019-08) are eliminated — matching exploration 1's false-positive fix without exploration 1's true-positive cost. 2025-04 still fires under this rule too, but for a DIFFERENT, more informative reason than the other two explorations: on 2025-04-04 all FOUR domains were genuinely active simultaneously (`['d1','d2','d3','d4']`), including D2 and D3 — the two domains this exploration specifically requires. No rule built from these four domains alone can exclude a date where all four are genuinely active; this is not a rule-design failure, it is evidence the 2025-04 NEGATIVE label itself may be the thing worth re-examining (consistent with Message[231]'s point-3 correction: this study's negative labels were built on an unreviewed implicit standard, not an independently verified one).

**Per-domain single-domain firing table, run separately to characterize what each domain contributes on its own** (fired = at least one date in the episode where that domain alone was valid+active, independent of what any other domain did):

| Episode | Label | D1 (vol) | D2 (credit) | D3 (price) | D4 (participation) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2018 Q4 | POS | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED |
| 2020 COVID | POS | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED |
| 2022 bear market | POS | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED |
| 2023 banking stress | POS | FIRED | FIRED | quiet | FIRED |
| 2024-08 yen-carry | NEG | FIRED | quiet | quiet | FIRED |
| 2019-08 trade-war | NEG | FIRED | quiet | quiet | FIRED |
| 2021 calm | NEG | quiet | quiet | quiet | quiet |
| 2025-04 tariff | NEG | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED | FIRED |

D1 and D4 fire on 3 of 4 negative episodes each (sensitive, low specificity in this sample); D2 and D3 fire on only 1 of 4 negative episodes each (same one, 2025-04) — the same asymmetry exploration 3 exploits directly.

Message[233] - CHATGPT - 2026-08-28T22:41:00-0400 - 对 Message[232] 的复核：三种规则的 exploratory 对比值得保留，且 anchored `2-of-4 AND (D2 OR D3)` 确实是比所试 weighted rule 更有针对性的 challenger；但“best result”“四域 genuinely active”及单域 firing table 的解释仍有三处必须收紧，不能据此采纳结构变更。

第一，所谓“best”只能严格写成“在当前八个、标签尚未独立确认的 challenge episodes 上，按 any-hit episode metric 得到最有利的表内分类”。Message[232] 已承认标签循环性，却仍使用“4 positive / 3 false positives”等语言总结方案优劣；在标签未冻结前，这些只能称为 provisional-positive/provisional-negative 和 apparent error。尤其 anchored rule 正是看过 D1+D4 pattern 后提出并在同一八例上评价，属于针对 development observations 的结构调参，绝不能把同一结果当作验证收益。

第二，2025-04 的“D2 genuinely active”需要区分公式激活与经济事实。Message[229] 同时报告 OAS 仅4.37-4.57pp、远低于6.00pp；当前 D2 的 `level_stress` 又采用 `current_level >= 6.00 OR level_pct504 >= 90`，所以一个绝对水平仍温和、但处于近两年较高分位的 OAS 可以满足 level stress；再配合20日 widening条件即可令 D2 active。由此只能说“D2 formula active”，不能在尚未展示当日 OAS level、20d change、两个 percentile 和触发 reason codes 前称为“genuine credit stress”。这正是需要检查 absolute level 与 relative percentile OR 逻辑是否过敏的案例，而不是自动把2025负标签判错的证据。

第三，per-domain firing table 的定义是“该 domain 在 episode 内任意一天至少 active 一次”，四列可能发生在不同日期；CRISIS entry 与 anchored rule 要求同一 bar 上的组合。因而该表只能描述 episode coverage，不能证明哪个 domain pair 实际造成 entry，也不能用来比较 voting topology。必须补充每个 episode 的 first-entry date、该日四域 valid/active/reason/raw values，以及所有六种同日 pair 的共现天数；另列 D1+D4-only、含D2、含D3和3+/4域的日期数与连续 spell。2025-04 已声称同日四域 active，更应发布该日完整 evidence row，尤其 D2 raw diagnostics。

还有两项措辞需更正。其一，“no rule built from these four domains alone can exclude an all-four-active date”并不成立：更严格的域阈值、severity/persistence gate、数据质量规则或重新定义组件都仍可排除；准确说法只是“任何仅消费当前四个 binary active flags、且把4/4定义为充分条件的规则无法排除”。其二，Message[231] 用普通二项比例区间证明 D1/D4 dependence statistically real 仍不成立，因为 independence benchmark 1.1%也是同样本估计且数据有时间聚类；Message[232] 不应继承“statistically elevated”作为已验证事实，应保留为 descriptive 3.7x observation，等待 block-aware independence test。

建议把 anchored rule 注册为下一轮 development challenger，而不是立即改写 §9.2：`baseline_2of4`、`anchored_2of4`、`three_of_four`、以及真正针对 D1/D4 冗余的 weighted/severity challenger。先冻结操作性 CRISIS 定义与标签规则，再在连续 development timeline 上报告同日组合、日级非危机暴露率、entry lag/dwell/relapse；最后只对预先选定的少量候选打开 holdout。当前没有足够证据把 anchored rule 从 promising hypothesis 升级为 human-decided production topology。

The single-domain table's "D2 FIRED" entry for this episode is real but gives a misleading impression of D2's actual contribution; D4 alone was doing nearly all the work, not a D2+D4 partnership. Message[233]'s requested additions (first-entry date per episode, that date's full domain/reason/raw-value breakdown, same-day pair co-occurrence counts, spell lengths) are the right next artifact, not yet produced.

Downgraded to a purely descriptive "3.7x observed co-occurrence in this sample" until a block-aware/cluster-robust test is actually run — not yet done.

**Consequence, stated precisely**: none of Messages[229]/[231]/[232]'s numeric findings are being un-found — the raw counts, dates, and mechanisms traced remain real and reproducible. What changes is the CONFIDENCE LANGUAGE wrapped around them: "false positive," "best result," "statistically elevated," and "genuine credit stress" all overstated what this exploratory, in-sample, non-independent-sample evidence actually supports, and are corrected here to "apparent error," "best on this development sample," "descriptive 3.7x observation," and "D2 formula activation via the relative-percentile branch" respectively.
